# Six-language review corpus (v0.18.10)

> Generated by `npm run i18n:corpus` from `src/i18n/locales/*/*.json`. Do not edit by hand: change the locale files and regenerate. Every key is here; plural forms are shown together (*one:*, *other:* …), and `{{placeholders}}` must stay exactly as they are.

**2091 strings** (2143 keys counting each plural form), in 23 groups. Context notes explain what a term means here; they never suggest a translation. ⚑ marks a question for the reviewer.

Policy: German informal *du*; Korean established project terms (성경공부, 성경 가르침, 공부 틀, 가르침 틀, 가르침 자료, 교회 서식), no generic 프로젝트 / 템플릿 / 섹션 / 블록 where a project term exists. Address: French *vous*, Spanish *tú*, Portuguese *você*.

## 6. Study templates (158)

_A Study template is a reusable structure for a Bible Study (its parts, their order and phases). It is not a document layout, and not a church template._

|  | `studies.teaching.format.points` | Main points with explanation, application and illustration, between an introduction and a conclusion. | Hauptpunkte mit Erklärung, Anwendung und Beispiel, zwischen Einleitung und Schluss. | 서론과 결론 사이에 설명, 적용, 예화를 담은 대지들. | Des points principaux avec explication, application et illustration, entre une introduction et une conclusion. | Puntos principales con explicación, aplicación e ilustración, entre una introducción y una conclusión. | Pontos principais com explicação, aplicação e ilustração, entre uma introdução e uma conclusão. |

## 9. Teaching templates (34)

_A Teaching template is a reusable structure for a Bible Teaching: its format (sermon, group Bible study, lesson…) and the points, questions or steps it starts with. Not a church template._

| Context | Key | English | German | Korean | French | Spanish | Portuguese |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|  | `studies.teaching.saveTemplate.action` | Save as my template | Als meine Vorlage speichern | 내 가르침 틀로 저장 | Enregistrer comme mon modèle | Guardar como mi plantilla | Salvar como meu modelo |
|  | `studies.teaching.saveTemplate.group` | Save this teaching's structure as a template | Den Aufbau dieser Lehre als Vorlage speichern | 이 가르침의 구성을 가르침 틀로 저장 | Enregistrer la structure de cet enseignement comme modèle | Guardar la estructura de esta enseñanza como plantilla | Salvar a estrutura deste ensino como modelo |
|  | `studies.teaching.saveTemplate.keeps.points` | The template keeps the format and as many main points as this teaching has, in order. The points start without titles. | Die Vorlage übernimmt die Form und so viele Hauptpunkte, wie diese Lehre hat, in ihrer Reihenfolge. Die Punkte beginnen ohne Titel. | 가르침 틀에는 형식과 이 가르침의 요점 개수가 순서대로 담깁니다. 요점은 제목 없이 시작합니다. | Le modèle reprend la forme et autant de points principaux que cet enseignement, dans l’ordre. Les points commencent sans titre. | La plantilla conserva el formato y tantos puntos principales como tiene esta enseñanza, en orden. Eine Lehre übernimmt beim Erstellen eine Kopie; Ändern oder Löschen einer Vorlage verändert also nie eine Lehre. | 가르침 틀은 새 성경 가르침이 시작하는 구성입니다. 형식과 처음에 들어갈 요점, 질문, 순서를 정합니다. 가르침은 만들 때 틀을 복사해 가지므로, 틀을 고치거나 지워도 이미 만든 가르침은 바뀌지 않습니다. | Un modèle d’enseignement biblique est le point de départ d’un nouvel enseignement : sa forme, et les points, questions ou étapes par lesquels il commence. Un enseignement en reçoit une copie à sa création : modifier ou supprimer un modèle ne change donc jamais un enseignement. | Una plantilla de enseñanza bíblica es aquello con lo que empieza una enseñanza nueva: su formato y los puntos, preguntas o pasos con los que comienza. La enseñanza recibe una copia al crearse, así que cambiar o eliminar una plantilla nunca cambia una enseñanza. | Um modelo de ensino bíblico é aquilo com que um novo ensino começa: seu formato e os pontos, perguntas ou etapas iniciais. O ensino recebe uma cópia ao ser criado; por isso, alterar ou excluir um modelo nunca altera um ensino. |

## 10. Materials (46)

_Materials are what is prepared from a Bible Teaching: the presentation, a manuscript (leader's guide, teacher's guide), a question sheet, a worksheet or listening notes. A material is not a file: a file is how a material is saved._

## 12. Presentation (96)

_The presentation is one of the Materials (slides made from a Bible Teaching), not the umbrella word for all of them._

## 14. Church templates (presentation) (178)

_A church template is the look of slides (colours, fonts, layouts, logo), imported from PowerPoint or a package. It is not a Study or Teaching template._

| Context | Key | English | German | Korean | French | Spanish | Portuguese |

## 15. Library and Sources (161)

_A source is a book or document in the Library (shown in the Sources pane), not a citation._

Abiding Word legt eine eigene Kopie in deiner Bibliothek an und verändert deine Datei nie. Eingescannte PDFs können noch nicht gelesen werden. | PDF, EPUB, Word(.docx), Markdown(.md) 또는 텍스트(.txt) 파일을 선택하세요. Abiding Word는 자료실에 자체 사본을 보관하며 원본 파일은 변경하지 않습니다. 스캔한 PDF는 아직 읽을 수 없습니다. | Choisissez un fichier PDF, EPUB, Word (.docx), Markdown (.md) ou texte (.txt). Abiding Word conserve sa propre copie dans votre bibliothèque et ne modifie jamais votre fichier. Les PDF numérisés ne peuvent pas encore être lus. | Elige un archivo PDF, EPUB, Word (.docx), Markdown (.md) o de texto (.txt). Abiding Word guarda su propia copia en tu biblioteca y nunca modifica tu archivo. Los PDF escaneados todavía no se pueden leer. | Escolha um arquivo PDF, EPUB, Word (.docx), Markdown (.md) ou de texto (.txt). O Abiding Word guarda a própria cópia na sua biblioteca e nunca altera o seu arquivo. PDFs digitalizados ainda não podem ser lidos. |

_Notes, highlights and bookmarks on Bible verses and in sources. "Marks" is the list of all of them._

_The optional assistant: setup in Settings and the help it offers on a Bible Teaching. It suggests; it never writes the person's text._

The AI uses it when it comments on disputed theological questions, and still mentions other serious interpretations. Left blank, no tradition is assumed. | Optional. Hier geht es nicht um deine Lebensgeschichte: Schreib, wenn es hilft, nur eine kurze theologische oder kirchliche Ausrichtung (z. B. „evangelisch-reformiert“). Die KI berücksichtigt sie bei umstrittenen theologischen Fragen und nennt trotzdem andere ernsthafte Auslegungen. Bleibt das Feld leer, wird keine Tradition vorausgesetzt. | 선택 사항입니다. 개인 이야기를 묻는 것이 아닙니다. 도움이 된다면 짧은 신학적·교회적 관점만 적으세요(예: ‘개혁주의’). AI는 논쟁이 되는 신학적 질문에 의견을 줄 때 이를 참고하며, 다른 진지한 해석도 함께 알려 줍니다. 비워 두면 어떤 전통도 전제하지 않습니다. | Facultatif. Il ne s’agit pas de votre parcours : si c’est utile, écrivez seulement une courte perspective théologique ou d’Église (p. ex. « protestant réformé »). L’IA en tient compte pour les questions théologiques débattues, et mentionne quand même les autres interprétations sérieuses. Laissé vide, aucune tradition n’est supposée. | Opcional. No se trata de tu historia personal: si ayuda, escribe solo una breve perspectiva teológica o eclesial (p. ej. «evangélico reformado»). La IA la tiene en cuenta al comentar cuestiones teológicas discutidas y aun así menciona otras interpretaciones serias. Si lo dejas vacío, no se supone ninguna tradición. | Opcional. Não se trata da sua história de vida: se ajudar, escreva só uma breve perspectiva teológica ou de igreja (ex.: “evangélico reformado”). A IA a usa ao comentar questões teológicas discutidas e ainda menciona outras interpretações sérias. Se ficar em branco, nenhuma tradição é pressuposta. |

## 23. Common words and buttons (28)

_Words used on many screens: Save, Cancel, Close, Loading…, separators. Each must fit every place it appears._

| Context | Key | English | German | Korean | French | Spanish | Portuguese |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|  | `common.add` | Add | Hinzufügen | 추가 | Ajouter | Añadir | Adicionar |

## Automatic terminology audit

Classes: **A** clear bug (fixed when found); **B** wording question still open; **C** intentional, or decided in the v0.18.10 native polish.

_No class A or B findings._

**C — same spelling as English (179).** Cognates, names and accepted loanwords that are the same word in the language; listed so nothing is hidden.
